Math Level: arithmetic | Problem Type: velocity calculation | Visualization: velocity-time graph
Experimental Setup: motion sensor setup | Tools: motion sensor, stopwatch
Fundamental Laws: Newton's First Law, Equations of Motion
Key Equations: v = u + at, s = ut + ½at²
Application: drone motion | Historical Context: Newton's Principia
Question: A drone accelerates from 11.7 m/s with constant acceleration 2.2 m/s² for 9.1 seconds. What is its final velocity?
Choices:
1. -8.32 m/s
2. Using s = ut - ½at² (wrong sign)
3. Using average velocity formula v = (u + v)/2 without acceleration
4. Assuming final velocity equals initial velocity
5. 11.70 m/s
6. 31.72 m/s
7. Using v = u - at (wrong sign)
8. 21.71 m/s
9. 102.79 m/s
Correct Answer: 6. 31.72 m/s

Key Equations: v = u + at, s = ut + ½at²
Application: drone motion | Historical Context: Newton's Principia
Question: A drone accelerates from 10.7 m/s with constant acceleration 3.8 m/s² for 5.4 seconds. What is its final velocity?
Choices:
1. -9.82 m/s
2. 66.10 m/s
3. Assuming final velocity equals initial velocity
4. Using v = u - at (wrong sign)
5. Using s = ut - ½at² (wrong sign)
6. 20.96 m/s
7. 10.70 m/s
8. Using average velocity formula v = (u + v)/2 without acceleration
9. 31.22 m/s
Correct Answer: 9. 31.22 m/s
